Serialize every host-side proxy call through this lock.\n47\t_CAIDO_CALL_LOCK = asyncio.Lock()\n48\t\n49\t\n50\tdef _ctx_client(ctx: RunContextWrapper) -> Client | None:\n51\t    inner = ctx.context if isinstance(ctx.context, dict) else {}\n52\t    return inner.get(\"caido_client\")\n53\t\n54\t\n55\tasync def _call[T](client: Client, fn: Callable[[Client], Awaitable[T]]) -> T:\n56\t    \"\"\"Run ``fn`` against the shared client, serialized under ``_CAIDO_CALL_LOCK``.\"\"\"\n57\t    async with _CAIDO_CALL_LOCK:\n58\t        return await fn(client)\n59\t\n60\t\n61\tdef _to_tool_json(value: Any) -> Any:\n62\t    \"\"\"Recursively convert SDK dataclasses/Pydantic objects to tool JSON values.\"\"\"\n63\t    if value is None or isinstance(value, str | int | float | bool):\n64\t        return value\n65\t    if isinstance(value, bytes):\n66\t        try:\n67\t            return value.decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\n68\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n69\t            return value.hex()\n70\t    if isinstance(value, datetime):\n71\t        return value.isoformat()\n72\t    if is_dataclass(value) and not isinstance(value, type):\n73\t        return {k: _to_tool_json(v) for k, v in dataclasses.asdict(value).items()}\n74\t    if hasattr(value, \"model_dump\"):\n75\t        return _to_tool_json(value.model_dump())\n76\t    if isinstance(value, dict):\n77\t        return {str(k): _to_tool_json(v) for k, v in value.items()}\n78\t    if isinstance(value, list | tuple | set):\n79\t        return [_to_tool_json(v) for v in value]\n80\t    return str(value)\n81\t\n82\t\n83\tdef _no_client() -> str:\n84\t    return json.dumps(\n85\t        {\"success\": False, \"error\": \"Caido client not available in run context\"},\n86\t        ensure_ascii=False,\n87\t        default=str,\n88\t    )\n89\t\n90\t\n91\tdef _err(name: str, exc: Exception) -> str:\n92\t    logger.exception(\"%s failed\", name)\n93\t    return json.dumps(\n94\t        {\"success\": False, \"error\": f\"{name} failed: {exc}\"},\n95\t        ensure_ascii=False,\n96\t        default=str,\n97\t    )\n98\t\n99\t\n100\t@function_tool(timeout=120)\n101\tasync def list_requests(\n102\t    ctx: RunContextWrapper,\n103\t    httpql_filter: str | None = None,\n104\t    first: int = 50,\n105\t    after: str | None = None,\n106\t    sort_by: SortBy = \"timestamp\",\n107\t    sort_order: SortOrder = \"desc\",\n108\t    scope_id: str | None = None,\n109\t) -> str:\n110\t    \"\"\"List captured HTTP requests from the Caido proxy with HTTPQL filtering.\n111\t\n112\t    Caido HTTPQL syntax (operators differ by field type):\n113\t\n114\t    - **Integer fields** (``resp.code``, ``req.port``, ``id``,\n115\t      ``roundtrip``) — ``eq``, ``gt``, ``gte``, ``lt``, ``lte``, ``ne``.\n116\t      Examples: ``resp.code.eq:200``, ``resp.code.gte:400``,\n117\t      ``req.port.eq:443``.\n118\t    - **Text/byte fields** (``req.method``, ``req.host``, ``req.path``,\n119\t      ``req.query``, ``req.ext``, ``req.raw``) — ``regex``, ``cont``\n120\t      (substring), ``eq``. Examples: ``req.method.eq:\"POST\"``,\n121\t      ``req.path.cont:\"/api/\"``, ``req.host.regex:\".*\\\\.example\\\\.com\"``.\n122\t    - **Date fields** (``req.created_at``) — ``gt``, ``lt`` with ISO\n123\t      timestamps: ``req.created_at.gt:\"2024-01-01T00:00:00Z\"``.\n124\t    - **Combine** with ``AND`` / ``OR``: ``req.method.eq:\"POST\" AND\n125\t      resp.code.gte:400``.\n126\t    - **Special**: ``source:intercept`` (only intercepted requests),\n127\t      ``preset:\"name\"``.\n128\t\n129\t    For sitemap-style tree traversal use HTTPQL filters: drill into a\n130\t    host with ``req.host.eq:\"example.com\"`` then narrow paths with\n131\t    ``req.path.cont:\"/api/\"``.\n132\t\n133\t    Pagination is cursor-based. Pass the ``end_cursor`` from the\n134\t    ``page_info`` of one call as ``after`` to the next.\n135\t\n136\t    Notes:\n137\t\n138\t    - HTTPQL has **no ``NOT`` operator**. Use the negated form of the\n139\t      operator instead: ``ne``, ``ncont``, ``nlike``, ``nregex``\n140\t      (e.g. ``req.path.ncont:\"/static\"`` to exclude static paths).\n141\t    - String values **must be quoted**; integer values **must not**.\n142\t      ``resp.code.eq:200`` is right; ``resp.code.eq:\"200\"`` is a parse\n143\t      error. Same rule for ``cont`` / ``regex`` strings.\n144\t    - A bare quoted string searches both ``req.raw`` and ``resp.raw``,\n145\t      handy for sensitive-data sweeps:\n146\t      ``\"password\" OR \"secret\" OR \"api_key\"``.\n147\t\n148\t    Args:\n149\t        httpql_filter: Caido HTTPQL query (optional).\n150\t        first: Number of entries to return (default 50).\n151\t        after: Cursor from a previous response's ``page_info.end_cursor``.\n152\t        sort_by: One of ``timestamp`` / ``host`` / ``method`` / ``path``\n153\t            / ``status_code`` / ``response_time`` / ``response_size``\n154\t            / ``source``.\n155\t        sort_order: ``asc`` or ``desc``.\n156\t        scope_id: Restrict to a Caido scope (managed via ``scope_rules``).\n157\t    \"\"\"\n158\t    client = _ctx_client(ctx)\n159\t    if client is None:\n160\t        return _no_client()\n161\t\n162\t    try:\n163\t        connection = await _call(\n164\t            client,\n165\t            lambda client: caido_api.list_requests_with_client(\n166\t                client,\n167\t                httpql_filter=httpql_filter,\n168\t                first=first,\n169\t                after=after,\n170\t                sort_by=sort_by,\n171\t                sort_order=sort_order,\n172\t                scope_id=scope_id,\n173\t            ),\n174\t        )\n175\t\n176\t        entries = []\n177\t        for edge in connection.edges:\n178\t            req = edge.node.request\n179\t            resp = edge.node.response\n180\t            response_payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None\n181\t            if resp is not None:\n182\t                response_payload = {\n183\t                    \"id\": resp.id,\n184\t                    \"status_code\": resp.status_code,\n185\t                    \"length\": resp.length,\n186\t                    \"created_at\": resp.created_at.isoformat(),\n187\t                }\n188\t                # Caido populates ``roundtripTime`` for some traffic sources\n189\t                # and leaves it as ``0`` for others (notably proxy captures\n190\t                # of upstream env-routed traffic). Surface the value only\n191\t                # when it's actually measured so the model doesn't waste\n192\t                # tokens reading a zero field on every entry.\n193\t                if resp.roundtrip_time:\n194\t                    response_payload[\"roundtrip_ms\"] = resp.roundtrip_time\n195\t            entries.append(\n196\t                {\n197\t                    \"cursor\": edge.cursor,\n198\t                    \"request\": {\n199\t                        \"id\": req.id,\n200\t                        \"host\": req.host,\n201\t                        \"port\": req.port,\n202\t                        \"method\": req.method,\n203\t                        \"path\": req.path,\n204\t                        \"query\": req.query,\n205\t                        \"is_tls\": req.is_tls,\n206\t                        \"created_at\": req.created_at.isoformat(),\n207\t                    },\n208\t                    \"response\": response_payload,\n209\t                },\n210\t            )\n211\t\n212\t        return json.dumps(\n213\t            {\n214\t                \"success\": True,\n215\t                \"entries\": entries,\n216\t                \"page_info\": {\n217\t                    \"has_next_page\": connection.page_info.has_next_page,\n218\t                    \"has_previous_page\": connection.page_info.has_previous_page,\n219\t                    \"start_cursor\": connection.page_info.start_cursor,\n220\t                    \"end_cursor\": connection.page_info.end_cursor,\n221\t                },\n222\t            },\n223\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n224\t            default=str,\n225\t        )\n226\t    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001\n227\t        return _err(\"list_requests\", exc)\n228\t\n229\t\n230\t@function_tool(timeout=60)\n231\tasync def view_request(\n232\t    ctx: RunContextWrapper,\n233\t    request_id: str,\n234\t    part: RequestPart = \"request\",\n235\t    search_pattern: str | None = None,\n236\t    page: int = 1,\n237\t    page_size: int = 50,\n238\t) -> str:\n239\t    \"\"\"View a captured request or its response, optionally regex-searched.\n240\t\n241\t    Two modes:\n242\t\n243\t    - **With** ``search_pattern`` (compact regex hits) — returns up to 20\n244\t      matches with ``before`` / ``after`` context and position. Useful\n245\t      for hunting reflected input, leaked URLs, hidden parameters.\n246\t    - **Without** ``search_pattern`` (full content with line pagination)\n247\t      — returns the page of raw content plus ``has_more`` flag.\n248\t\n249\t    Common search patterns:\n250\t\n251\t    - API endpoints: ``/api/[a-zA-Z0-9._/-]+``\n252\t    - URLs: ``https?://[^\\\\s<>\"']+``\n253\t    - Query parameters: ``[?&][a-zA-Z0-9_]+=([^&\\\\s<>\"']+)``\n254\t    - Specific input reflection: search for the value you submitted.\n255\t\n256\t    Args:\n257\t        request_id: Request ID from ``list_requests``.\n258\t        part: ``\"request\"`` or ``\"response\"``.\n259\t        search_pattern: Optional regex; switches the response shape to\n260\t            compact hits.\n261\t        page: 1-indexed page number (only when no ``search_pattern``).\n262\t        page_size: Lines per page.\n263\t    \"\"\"\n264\t    client = _ctx_client(ctx)\n265\t    if client is None:\n266\t part=part),\n272\t        )\n273\t        if result is None:\n274\t            return json.dumps(\n275\t                {\"success\": False, \"error\": f\"Request {request_id} not found\"},\n276\t                ensure_ascii=False,\n277\t                default=str,\n278\t            )\n279\t\n280\t        raw_bytes = (\n281\t            result.request.raw\n282\t            if part == \"request\"\n283\t            else (result.response.raw if result.response is not None else None)\n284\t        )\n285\t        if raw_bytes is None:\n286\t            return json.dumps(\n287\t     return json.dumps(\n298\t                _format_search_hits(content, search_pattern),\n299\t                ensure_ascii=False,\n300\t                default=str,\n301\t Any]:\n313\t    try:\n314\t        regex = re.compile(pattern)\n315\t    except re.error as exc:\n316\t        return {\"success\": False, \"error\": f\"Invalid regex: {exc}\"}\n317\t\n318\t    hits = []\n319\t    for match in regex.finditer(content):\n320\t        start, end = match.span()\n321\t        before = content[max(0, start - 40) : start]\n322\t        after = content[end : end + 40]\n323\t        hits.append(\n324\t            {\n325\t                \"match\": match.group(0),\n326\t                \"position\": start,\n327\t                \"before\": before,\n328\t                \"after\": after,\n329\t            },\n330\t        )\n331\t        if len(hits) >= 20:\n332\t            break\n333\t\n334\t    return {\"success\": True, \"hits\": hits, \"total_hits\": len(hits)}\n335\t\n336\t\n337\tdef _format_text_page(content: str, *, page: int, page_size: int) -> dict[str, Any]:\n338\t    lines = content.splitlines()\n339\t    start = max(0, (page - 1) * page_size)\n340\t    end = start + page_size\n341\t    return {\n342\t        \"success\": True,\n343\t        \"content\": \"\\n\".join(lines[start:end]),\n344\t        \"page\": page,\n345\t        \"page_size\": page_size,\n346\t        \"total_lines\": len(lines),\n347\t        \"has_more\": end < len(lines),\n348\t    }\n349\t\n350\t\n351\t@function_tool(timeout=120, strict_mode=False)\n352\tasync def repeat_request(\n353\t    ctx: RunContextWrapper,\n354\t    request_id: str,\n355\t    modifications: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n356\t) -> str:\n357\t Inherits everything from the original request\n369\t    (headers, cookies, auth, method, URL) and overlays only the fields\n370\t    you specify in ``modifications``.\n371\t\n372\t    Args:\n373\t        request_id: ID of the original request (from ``list_requests``).\n374\t        modifications: Patch dict. body=modified[\"body\"],\n401\t        )\n402\t        return await caido_api.replay_send_raw(client, raw=raw, connection=connection)\n403\t\n404\t    try:\n405\t        replay = await _call(client, scope_id: Required for ``get`` / ``update`` / ``delete``.\n555\t        scope_name: Required for ``create`` / ``update``.\n556\t    \"\"\"\n557\t    client = _ctx_client(ctx)\n558\t    if client is {"parentUuid":"43c18544-45ee-49f7-a7ce-13d29b700455","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"3bfd3d93-3d8c-4d5a-8244-149007afff7e","agentId":"a1642ebb3bfdf63d1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0161J1iC5ZVD9bQUmYPjKA2p","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Multi-agent graph tools backed by AgentCoordinator.\"\"\"\n2\t\n3\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n4\t\n5\timport asyncio\n6\timport json\n7\timport logging\n8\timport uuid\n9\tfrom collections import Counter\n10\tfrom datetime import UTC, datetime\n11\tfrom typing import Any, Literal, get_args\n12\t\n13\tfrom agents import RunContextWrapper, function_tool\n14\t\n15\tfrom strix.core.agents import Status, coordinator_from_context\n16\tfrom strix.skills import validate_requested_skills\n17\t\n18\t\n19\t_ACTIVE_STATUSES: frozenset[str] = frozenset({\"running\", \"waiting\"})\n20\t\n21\t\n22\tlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n23\t\n24\t\n25\tdef _ctx(ctx: RunContextWrapper) -> dict[str, Any]:\n26\t    return ctx.context if isinstance(ctx.context, dict) else {}\n27\t\n28\t\n29\tdef _render_completion_report(\n30\t    *,\n31\t    agent_name: str,\n32\t    agent_id: str,\n33\t    task: str,\n34\t    success: bool,\n35\t    result_summary: str,\n36\t    findings: list[str],\n37\t    recommendations: list[str],\n38\t) -> str:\n39\t    \"\"\"Render a child's completion report as plain structured text.\n40\t\n41\t    Goes into the parent's SDK session with coordinator-added sender\n42\t    metadata, so this body just carries the contents. No XML — no\n43\t    escaping concerns, no parser ambiguity.\n44\t    \"\"\"\n45\t    status = \"SUCCESS\" if success else \"FAILED\"\n46\t    completion_time = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()\n47\t\n48\t    lines: list[str] = [\n49\t        f\"== Completion report from {agent_name} ({agent_id}) ==\",\n50\t        f\"Status: {status}\",\n51\t        f\"Time: {completion_time}\",\n52\t    ]\n53\t    if task:\n54\t        lines.append(f\"Task: {task}\")\n55\t    lines.append(\"\")\n56\t    lines.append(\"Summary:\")\n57\t    lines.append(result_summary or \"(none)\")\n58\t    if findings:\n59\t        lines.append(\"\")\n60\t        lines.append(\"Findings:\")\n61\t        lines.extend(f\"- {f}\" for f in findings)\n62\t    if recommendations:\n63\t        lines.append(\"\")\n64\t        lines.append(\"Recommendations:\")\n65\t        lines.extend(f\"- {r}\" for r in recommendations)\n66\t    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n67\t\n68\t\n69\t@function_tool(timeout=30)\n70\tasync def view_agent_graph(ctx: RunContextWrapper) -> str:\n71\t    \"\"\"Print the multi-agent tree — every agent, its parent, its status.\n72\t\n73\t    Use before spawning a new agent (don't duplicate work — check whether\n74\t    something specialized for that task already exists) and any time you\n75\t    want a snapshot of who's still ``running`` / ``waiting`` /\n76\t    ``completed`` / ``crashed`` / ``stopped``. Output is an indented\n77\t    bullet list with status in brackets; the agent that called this tool\n78\t    is marked ``← you``.\n79\t    \"\"\"\n80\t    inner = _ctx(ctx)\n81\t    coordinator = coordinator_from_context(inner)\n82\t    me = inner.get(\"agent_id\")\n83\t    if coordinator is None:\n84\t        return json.dumps(\n85\t            {\"success\": False, \"error\": \"Agent coordinator not initialized in context\"},\n86\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n87\t            default=str,\n88\t        )\n89\t\n90\t    parent_of, statuses, names = await coordinator.graph_snapshot()\n91\t\n92\t    lines: list[str] = []\n93\t\n94\t    def render(aid: str, depth: int) -> None:\n95\t        status = statuses.get(aid, \"?\")\n96\t        marker = \"  ← you\" if aid == me else \"\"\n97\t        lines.append(f\"{'  ' * depth}- {names.get(aid, aid)} ({aid}) [{status}]{marker}\")\n98\t        for child, p in parent_of.items():\n99\t            if p == aid:\n100\t                render(child, depth + 1)\n101\t\n102\t    roots = [aid for aid, parent in parent_of.items() if parent is None]\n103\t    for root in roots:\n104\t        render(root, 0)\n105\t\n106\t    counts = Counter(statuses.values())\n107\t    summary: dict[str, int] = {\"total\": len(parent_of)}\n108\t    for status_name in get_args(Status):\n109\t        summary[status_name] = counts.get(status_name, 0)\n110\t    return json.dumps(\n111\t        {\n112\t            \"success\": True,\n113\t            \"graph_structure\": \"\\n\".join(lines) or \"(no agents)\",\n114\t            \"summary\": summary,\n115\t        },\n116\t        ensure_ascii=False,\n117\t        default=str,\n118\t    )\n119\t\n120\t\n121\t@function_tool(timeout=30)\n122\tasync def send_message_to_agent(\n123\t    ctx: RunContextWrapper,\n124\t    target_agent_id: str,\n125\t    message: str,\n126\t    message_type: Literal[\"query\", \"instruction\", \"information\"] = \"information\",\n127\t    priority: Literal[\"low\", \"normal\", \"high\", \"urgent\"] = \"normal\",\n128\t) -> str:\n129\t    \"\"\"Send a message to another agent's inbox — sparingly.\n130\t\n131\t    Inter-agent messages are appended to the target's SDK session and\n132\t    interrupt any active target turn so the next run cycle sees them.\n133\t    Use only when essential:\n134\t\n135\t    - Sharing a discovered finding/credential another agent needs.\n136\t    - Asking a specialist a focused question.\n137\t    - Coordinating who covers what (avoid overlap).\n138\t    - Telling a child to wrap up or change course.\n139\t\n140\t    **Don't** use for routine \"hello/status\" pings, for context the\n141\t    target already has (children inherit parent history), or when\n142\t    parent/child completion via ``agent_finish`` already covers the\n143\t    flow. Messages to any registered agent wake it, regardless of\n144\t    status, so a follow-up can restart a completed/stopped/failed agent.\n145\t\n146\t    Args:\n147\t        target_agent_id: Recipient's 8-char id.\n148\t        message: The full message body. Be specific — include payloads,\n149\t            URLs, or what you want them to do, not just headlines.\n150\t        message_type: ``query`` (you want a reply), ``instruction``\n151\t            (you're directing them), ``information`` (FYI, no reply\n152\t            expected). Default ``information``.\n153\t        priority: ``low`` / ``normal`` / ``high`` / ``urgent``.\n154\t    \"\"\"\n155\t    inner = _ctx(ctx)\n156\t    coordinator = coordinator_from_context(inner)\n157\t    me = inner.get(\"agent_id\")\n158\t    if coordinator is None or me is None:\n159\t        return json.dumps(\n160\t            {\"success\": False, \"error\": \"Agent coordinator or agent_id missing in context\"},\n161\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n162\t            default=str,\n163\t        )\n164\t    if target_agent_id == me:\n165\t        return json.dumps(\n166\t            {\n167\t                \"success\": False,\n168\t                \"error\": (\n169\t                    \"Cannot send a message to yourself; use `think` to record a \"\n170\t                    \"private note, or `agent_finish` / `finish_scan` to terminate\"\n171\t                ),\n172\t            },\n173\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n174\t            default=str,\n175\t        )\n176\t\n177\t    msg_id = f\"msg_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}\"\n178\t    delivered = await coordinator.send(\n179\t        target_agent_id,\n180\t        {\n181\t            \"id\": msg_id,\n182\t            \"from\": me,\n183\t            \"content\": message,\n184\t            \"type\": message_type,\n185\t            \"priority\": priority,\n186\t        },\n187\t    )\n188\t    if not delivered:\n189\t        return json.dumps(\n190\t            {\n191\t                \"success\": False,\n192\t                \"error\": f\"Target agent '{target_agent_id}' not found or message delivery failed\",\n193\t            },\n194\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n195\t            default=str,\n196\t        )\n197\t    return json.dumps(\n198\t        {\n199\t            \"success\": True,\n200\t            \"message_id\": msg_id,\n201\t            \"target_agent_id\": target_agent_id,\n202\t            \"delivery_status\": \"delivered\",\n203\t        },\n204\t        ensure_ascii=False,\n205\t        default=str,\n206\t    )\n207\t\n208\t\n209\tdef _session_items_payload(items: list[Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n210\t    payload: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n211\t    for item in items:\n212\t        if isinstance(item, dict):\n213\t            role = item.get(\"role\")\n214\t            content = item.get(\"content\")\n215\t            payload.append({\"role\": role, \"content\": content})\n216\t        else:\n217\t            payload.append({\"content\": str(item)})\n218\t    return payload\n219\t\n220\t\n221\t@function_tool(timeout=601)\n222\tasync def wait_for_message(  # noqa: PLR0911\n223\t    ctx: RunContextWrapper,\n224\t    reason: str = \"Waiting for messages from other agents\",\n225\t    timeout_seconds: int = 600,\n226\t) -> str:\n227\t    \"\"\"Pause this agent until a message lands in its inbox (or timeout).\n228\t\n229\t    Use when you have nothing useful to do until a child/peer responds\n230\t    — typically after spawning subagents and you want to wait for\n231\t    their completion reports. The agent automatically resumes when any\n232\t    message arrives, so pick a ``timeout_seconds`` proportional to the\n233\t    work you're awaiting.\n234\t\n235\t    **Critical caveats:**\n236\t\n237\t    - **Never** call this if you finished your own task and have **no**\n238\t      child agents running — that's a permanent stall. Call\n239\t      ``finish_scan`` (root) or ``agent_finish`` (subagent) instead.\n240\t    - If you're waiting on an agent that **isn't your child**, message\n241\t      it first asking it to ping you when done — otherwise it has no\n242\t      reason to send to your inbox and you'll wait the full timeout.\n243\t    - Children update the parent automatically via ``agent_finish``\n244\t      → no extra coordination needed.\n245\t\n246\t    Args:\n247\t        reason: One-line note shown in graph snapshots while you're\n248\t            waiting (helps a human or sibling agent debug who's stuck\n249\t            on what).\n250\t        timeout_seconds: Max seconds to wait (default 600). This is only\n251\t            a cap — the tool returns the INSTANT a message arrives, so a\n252\t            larger value never makes you wait longer when the reply does\n253\t            come. Right-size it to what you're waiting on: a short wait\n254\t            (e.g. 10-60s) for a quick ack or a small/fast subtask, and a\n255\t            longer one (e.g. ~100-200s) only for genuinely long-running\n256\t            work (deep recon, exploitation, a full sub-scan). The cap only\n257\t            bites when the expected message never arrives — so an oversized\n258\t            timeout on a trivial wait just strands you idle until it\n259\t            elapses. On timeout the tool returns and you decide whether to\n260\t            keep working or wait again. (Applies to autonomous multi-agent\n261\t            runs; in interactive/chat sessions the agent instead parks until\n262\t            a message arrives and this cap is not enforced.)\n263\t    \"\"\"\n264\t    inner = _ctx(ctx)\n265\t    coordinator = coordinator_from_context(inner)\n266\t    me = inner.get(\"agent_id\")\n267\t    interactive = bool(inner.get(\"interactive\", False))\n268\t    if coordinator is None or me is None:\n269\t        return json.dumps(\n270\t            {\"success\": False, \"error\": \"Agent coordinator or agent_id missing in context\"},\n271\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n272\t            default=str,\n273\t        )\n274\t\n275\t    async with coordinator._lock:\n276\t        stopped = coordinator.statuses.get(me) == \"stopped\"\n277\t    if stopped:\n278\t        return json.dumps(\n279\t            {\n280\t                \"success\": True,\n281\t                \"wait_outcome\": \"stopped\",\n282\t                \"reason\": reason,\n283\t                \"note\": \"Wait ended because this agent is stopped.\",\n284\t            },\n285\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n286\t            default=str,\n287\t        )\n288\t\n289\t    pending, items = await coordinator.consume_pending(me, include_items=True)\n290\t    if pending > 0:\n291\t        await coordinator.mark_running(me)\n292\t        return json.dumps(\n293\t            {\n294\t                \"success\": True,\n295\t                \"wait_outcome\": \"message_arrived\",\n296\t                \"pending_messages\": pending,\n297\t                \"messages\": _session_items_payload(items),\n298\t                \"reason\": reason,\n299\t            },\n300\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n301\t            default=str,\n302\t        )\n303\t\n304\t    if interactive:\n305\t        await coordinator.park_waiting(me)\n306\t        return json.dumps(\n307\t            {\n308\t                \"success\": True,\n309\t                \"wait_outcome\": \"waiting\",\n310\t                \"reason\": reason,\n311\t                \"note\": \"Agent parked; execution will resume when a message arrives.\",\n312\t            },\n313\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n314\t            default=str,\n315\t        )\n316\t\n317\t    await coordinator.park_waiting(me)\n318\t    try:\n319\t        await asyncio.wait_for(coordinator.wait_for_message(me), timeout_seconds)\n320\t    except TimeoutError:\n321\t        await coordinator.mark_running(me)\n322\t        return json.dumps(\n323\t            {\n324\t                \"success\": True,\n325\t                \"wait_outcome\": \"timeout\",\n326\t                \"timeout_seconds\": timeout_seconds,\n327\t                \"reason\": reason,\n328\t                \"note\": \"No messages within timeout — continue work or call agent_finish.\",\n329\t            },\n330\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n331\t            default=str,\n332\t        )\n333\t\n334\t    async with coordinator._lock:\n335\t        stopped = coordinator.statuses.get(me) == \"stopped\"\n336\t    if stopped:\n337\t        return json.dumps(\n338\t            {\n339\t                \"success\": True,\n340\t                \"wait_outcome\": \"stopped\",\n341\t                \"reason\": reason,\n342\t                \"note\": \"Wait ended because this agent is stopped.\",\n343\t            },\n344\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n345\t            default=str,\n346\t        )\n347\t\n348\t    pending, items = await coordinator.consume_pending(me, include_items=True)\n349\t    await coordinator.mark_running(me)\n350\t\n351\t    return json.dumps(\n352\t        {\n353\t            \"success\": True,\n354\t            \"wait_outcome\": \"message_arrived\",\n355\t            \"pending_messages\": pending,\n356\t            \"messages\": _session_items_payload(items),\n357\t            \"reason\": reason,\n358\t        },\n359\t        ensure_ascii=False,\n360\t        default=str,\n361\t    )\n362\t\n363\t\n364\t@function_tool(timeout=120)\n365\tasync def create_agent(\n366\t    ctx: RunContextWrapper,\n367\t    name: str,\n368\t    task: str,\n369\t    inherit_context: bool = True,\n370\t    skills: list[str] | None = None,\n371\t) -> str:\n372\t    \"\"\"Spawn a specialist child agent to run in parallel.\n373\t\n374\t    Decompose complex pentests by handing focused subtasks to dedicated\n375\t    children. The child runs asynchronously — the parent continues\n376\t    immediately and can ``wait_for_message`` later (or just keep\n377\t    working in parallel). When the child calls ``agent_finish``, its\n378\t    completion report lands in the parent's inbox.\n379\t\n380\t    **Before spawning, call ``view_agent_graph``** to confirm no\n381\t    existing agent already covers this scope — duplicate specialists\n382\t    waste turns and create coordination headaches.\n383\t\n384\t    **Specialization principles:**\n385\t\n386\t    - Most agents need at least one ``skill`` to be useful.\n387\t    - Aim for **1-3 related skills** per agent. Up to 5 only when the\n388\t      task genuinely spans them.\n389\t    - One skill = most focused (e.g., XSS-only). Five skills = upper\n390\t      bound.\n391\t    - Match the ``name`` to the focus (``XSS Specialist``,\n392\t      ``SQLi Validator``, ``Auth Specialist``).\n393\t\n394\t    **When to spawn vs do it yourself:**\n395\t\n396\t    - Spawn when the subtask is large, parallelizable, or needs\n397\t      different specialization than what you're already doing.\n398\t    - Don't spawn for trivial one-shot probes — just run the tool\n399\t      yourself.\n400\t\n401\t    Args:\n402\t        name: Human-readable child name (used in graph views and\n403\t            ``send_message_to_agent`` flows).\n404\t        task: Specific objective. Be concrete — what to test, what\n405\t            success looks like, any constraints.\n406\t        inherit_context: Default ``True``. The child receives the\n407\t            parent's input history as background; only set ``False``\n408\t            when starting a clean-slate task.\n409\t        skills: List of skill names (e.g. ``[\"xss\", \"sql_injection\"]``).\n410\t            Max 5; prefer 1-3.\n411\t    \"\"\"\n412\t    inner = _ctx(ctx)\n413\t    coordinator = coordinator_from_context(inner)\n414\t    parent_id = inner.get(\"agent_id\")\n415\t    spawner = inner.get(\"spawn_child_agent\")\n416\t\n417\t    if coordinator is None or parent_id is None:\n418\t        return json.dumps(\n419\t            {\"success\": False, \"error\": \"Agent coordinator or agent_id missing in context\"},\n420\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n421\t            default=str,\n422\t        )\n423\t    if not callable(spawner):\n424\t        return json.dumps(\n425\t            {\n426\t                \"success\": False,\n427\t                \"error\": \"Scan runner did not provide a child-agent spawner in context\",\n428\t            },\n429\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n430\t            default=str,\n431\t        )\n432\t\n433\t    skill_list = list(skills or [])\n434\t    skill_error = validate_requested_skills(skill_list)\n435\t    if skill_error:\n436\t        return json.dumps(\n437\t            {\"success\": False, \"error\": skill_error, \"agent_id\": None},\n438\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n439\t            default=str,\n440\t        )\n441\t\n442\t    parent_history = list(ctx.turn_input) if inherit_context and ctx.turn_input else []\n443\t    try:\n444\t        result = await spawner(\n445\t            parent_ctx=inner,\n446\t            name=name,\n447\t            task=task,\n448\t            skills=skill_list,\n449\t            parent_history=parent_history,\n450\t        )\n451\t    except Exception as e:\n452\t        logger.exception(\"create_agent: scan runner failed to spawn child '%s'\", name)\n453\t        return json.dumps(\n454\t            {\"success\": False, \"error\": f\"child spawn failed: {e!s}\"},\n455\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n456\t            default=str,\n457\t        )\n458\t\n459\t    logger.info(\n460\t        \"create_agent: spawned %s (%s) parent=%s skills=%d task_len=%d\",\n461\t        result.get(\"agent_id\"),\n462\t        name,\n463\t        parent_id or \"-\",\n464\t        len(skill_list),\n465\t        len(task or \"\"),\n466\t    )\n467\t\n468\t    return json.dumps(\n469\t        result,\n470\t        ensure_ascii=False,\n471\t        default=str,\n472\t    )\n473\t\n474\t\n475\t@function_tool(timeout=30)\n476\tasync def agent_finish(\n477\t    ctx: RunContextWrapper,\n478\t    result_summary: str,\n479\t    findings: list[str] | None = None,\n480\t    success: bool = True,\n481\t    report_to_parent: bool = True,\n482\t    final_recommendations: list[str] | None = None,\n483\t) -> str:\n484\t    \"\"\"Subagent termination — post a completion report to the parent.\n485\t\n486\t    **Subagents only.** Root agents must call ``finish_scan`` instead;\n487\t    this tool refuses to run for root agents. Calling this:\n488\t\n489\t    1. Marks the subagent as ``completed``.\n490\t    2. Posts a structured completion report to the parent's inbox\n491\t       (when ``report_to_parent`` is true).\n492\t    3. Stops this subagent's execution.\n493\t\n494\t    **Vulnerability findings must already be filed via\n495\t    ``create_vulnerability_report`` (or ``create_dependency_report``\n496\t    for known-CVE dependency/supply-chain findings) before calling\n497\t    this.** The ``findings`` field here is for narrative summary only\n498\t    — it does not register vulns in the scan report.\n499\t\n500\t    Write the summary as if the parent has no idea what you were\n501\t    doing: what did you test, what did you find/confirm/rule out,\n502\t    what's still open.\n503\t\n504\t    Args:\n505\t        result_summary: What you accomplished and discovered. Concrete\n506\t            and specific (URLs, parameters, payloads that worked).\n507\t        findings: Optional bullet list of confirmed observations. For\n508\t            credit-bearing vulnerabilities, file\n509\t            ``create_vulnerability_report`` first (or\n510\t            ``create_dependency_report`` for dependency CVEs); this is\n511\t            for narrative.\n512\t        success: Whether the assigned subtask was completed\n513\t            successfully. Default ``True``.\n514\t        report_to_parent: Whether to deliver the completion report to\n515\t            the parent's inbox. Default ``True``.\n516\t        final_recommendations: Optional next-step suggestions for the\n517\t            parent (e.g., \"prioritize testing X\", \"spawn an agent to\n518\t            cover Y\").\n519\t    \"\"\"\n520\t    inner = _ctx(ctx)\n521\t    coordinator = coordinator_from_context(inner)\n522\t    me = inner.get(\"agent_id\")\n523\t    if coordinator is None or me is None:\n524\t        return json.dumps(\n525\t            {\"success\": False, \"error\": \"Agent coordinator or agent_id missing in context\"},\n526\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n527\t            default=str,\n528\t        )\n529\t\n530\t    parent_id = inner.get(\"parent_id\")\n531\t    if parent_id is None:\n532\t        return json.dumps(\n533\t            {\n534\t                \"success\": False,\n535\t                \"error\": (\n536\t                    \"agent_finish is for subagents. Root/main agents must call finish_scan instead\"\n537\t                ),\n538\t            },\n539\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n540\t            default=str,\n541\t        )\n542\t\n543\t    parent_notified = False\n544\t    if report_to_parent:\n545\t        async with coordinator._lock:\n546\t            agent_name = coordinator.names.get(me, me)\n547\t        report = _render_completion_report(\n548\t            agent_name=agent_name,\n549\t            agent_id=me,\n550\t            task=str(inner.get(\"task\", \"\")),\n551\t            success=success,\n552\t            result_summary=result_summary,\n553\t            findings=list(findings or []),\n554\t            recommendations=list(final_recommendations or []),\n555\t        )\n556\t        await coordinator.send(\n557\t            parent_id,\n558\t            {\n559\t                \"id\": f\"report_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}\",\n560\t                \"from\": me,\n561\t                \"content\": report,\n562\t                \"type\": \"completion\",\n563\t                \"priority\": \"high\",\n564\t            },\n565\t        )\n566\t        parent_notified = True\n567\t\n568\t    logger.info(\n569\t        \"agent_finish: %s success=%s findings=%d parent_notified=%s\",\n570\t        me,\n571\t        success,\n572\t        len(findings or []),\n573\t        parent_notified,\n574\t    )\n575\t    await coordinator.set_status(me, \"completed\")\n576\t\n577\t    return json.dumps(\n578\t        {\n579\t            \"success\": True,\n580\t            \"agent_completed\": True,\n581\t            \"parent_notified\": parent_notified,\n582\t            \"agent_id\": me,\n583\t            \"summary\": result_summary,\n584\t            \"findings_count\": len(findings or []),\n585\t            \"has_recommendations\": bool(final_recommendations),\n586\t        },\n587\t        ensure_ascii=False,\n588\t        default=str,\n589\t    )\n590\t\n591\t\n592\t@function_tool(timeout=30)\n593\tasync def stop_agent(\n594\t    ctx: RunContextWrapper,\n595\t    target_agent_id: str,\n596\t    cascade: bool = True,\n597\t    reason: str = \"\",\n598\t) -> str:\n599\t    \"\"\"Gracefully stop a running agent (and optionally its descendants).\n600\t\n601\t    Uses the SDK's ``RunResultStreaming.cancel(mode=\"after_turn\")`` so the\n602\t    target's current turn finishes — including saving items to its\n603\t    session — before the run loop honors the cancel. The agent's\n604\t    interactive outer loop parks as ``stopped``; later user/peer\n605\t    messages can wake it again.\n606\t\n607\t    Use sparingly. Prefer ``send_message_to_agent`` (asking the agent\n608\t    to wrap up) for soft-stop scenarios. Reach for ``stop_agent`` when\n609\t    a child has gone off-track and won't self-correct.\n610\t\n611\t    Args:\n612\t        target_agent_id: The 8-char id from ``view_agent_graph`` /\n613\t            ``create_agent``. Cannot stop yourself.\n614\t        cascade: If ``True`` (default), also stop every descendant of\n615\t            ``target_agent_id`` leaves-first. ``False`` stops only the\n616\t            target.\n617\t        reason: Optional human-readable reason for the stop, surfaced\n618\t            in logs and telemetry.\n619\t    \"\"\"\n620\t    inner = _ctx(ctx)\n621\t    coordinator = coordinator_from_context(inner)\n622\t    me = inner.get(\"agent_id\")\n623\t    if coordinator is None or me is None:\n624\t        return json.dumps(\n625\t            {\"success\": False, \"error\": \"Agent coordinator or agent_id missing in context\"},\n626\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n627\t            default=str,\n628\t        )\n629\t    if target_agent_id == me:\n630\t        return json.dumps(\n631\t            {\n632\t                \"success\": False,\n633\t                \"error\": \"Cannot stop yourself; call agent_finish or finish_scan instead\",\n634\t            },\n635\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n636\t            default=str,\n637\t        )\n638\t    _, statuses, _ = await coordinator.graph_snapshot()\n639\t    if target_agent_id not in statuses:\n640\t        return json.dumps(\n641\t            {\"success\": False, \"error\": f\"Unknown agent_id: {target_agent_id}\"},\n642\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n643\t            default=str,\n644\t        )\n645\t\n646\t    current_status = statuses[target_agent_id]\n647\t    if current_status not in _ACTIVE_STATUSES:\n648\t        return json.dumps(\n649\t            {\n650\t                \"success\": False,\n651\t                \"error\": (\n652\t                    f\"Agent {target_agent_id} is already '{current_status}'; \"\n653\t                    \"stop_agent only acts on running/waiting agents — use \"\n654\t                    \"view_agent_graph to find still-active descendants and \"\n655\t                    \"stop them individually, or send_message_to_agent if you \"\n656\t                    \"want to wake this one with new instructions\"\n657\t                ),\n658\t                \"target_agent_id\": target_agent_id,\n659\t                \"current_status\": current_status,\n660\t            },\n661\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n662\t            default=str,\n663\t        )\n664\t\n665\t    if cascade:\n666\t        await coordinator.cancel_descendants_graceful(target_agent_id)\n667\t    else:\n668\t        await coordinator.request_stop(target_agent_id)\n669\t\n670\t    logger.info(\n671\t        \"stop_agent: target=%s cascade=%s reason=%r\",\n672\t        target_agent_id,\n673\t        cascade,\n674\t        reason,\n675\t    )\n676\t    return json.dumps(\n677\t        {\n678\t            \"success\": True,\n679\t            \"target_agent_id\": target_agent_id,\n680\t            \"cascade\": cascade,\n681\t            \"reason\": reason,\n682\t            \"note\": \"Cancellation is graceful — current turn completes first.\",\n683\t        },\n684\t        ensure_ascii=False,\n685\t        default=str,\n686\t    )\n687\t"}]},"uuid":"0a987f10-9dc2-41df-8f90-f7a60e398b2c","timestamp":"2026-07-20T01:21:57.453Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"43c18544-45ee-49f7-a7ce-13d29b700455","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Caido proxy helpers and sandbox-importable ``caido_api`` module.\"\"\"\n2\t\n3\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n4\t\n5\timport asyncio\n6\timport json\n7\timport os\n8\timport time\n9\timport urllib.request\n10\tfrom typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any, Literal\n11\tfrom urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlencode, urlparse, urlunparse\n12\t\n13\tfrom caido_sdk_client import Client, TokenAuthOptions\n14\tfrom caido_sdk_client.types import (\n15\t    ConnectionInfoInput,\n16\t    CreateScopeOptions,\n17\t    ReplaySendOptions,\n18\t    RequestGetOptions,\n19\t    UpdateScopeOptions,\n20\t)\n21\t\n22\t\n23\tif TYPE_CHECKING:\n24\t    from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable\n25\t\n26\t    from caido_sdk_client import Client as CaidoClient\n27\t\n28\t\n29\tRequestPart = Literal[\"request\", \"response\"]\n30\tSortBy = Literal[\n31\t    \"timestamp\",\n32\t    \"host\",\n33\t    \"method\",\n34\t    \"path\",\n35\t    \"status_code\",\n36\t    \"response_time\",\n37\t    \"response_size\",\n38\t    \"source\",\n39\t]\n40\tSortOrder = Literal[\"asc\", \"desc\"]\n41\tScopeAction = Literal[\"get\", \"list\", \"create\", \"update\", \"delete\"]\n42\tSitemapDepth = Literal[\"DIRECT\", \"ALL\"]\n43\t_SITEMAP_PAGE_SIZE = 30\n44\t\n45\t_DEFAULT_CAIDO_URL = \"http://127.0.0.1:48080\"\n46\t_CLIENT_CACHE: dict[str, Client] = {}\n47\t_CLIENT_LOCK = asyncio.Lock()\n48\t_REQ_FIELD_MAP: dict[SortBy, tuple[str, str]] = {\n49\t    \"timestamp\": (\"req\", \"created_at\"),\n50\t    \"host\": (\"req\", \"host\"),\n51\t    \"method\": (\"req\", \"method\"),\n52\t    \"path\": (\"req\", \"path\"),\n53\t    \"source\": (\"req\", \"source\"),\n54\t    \"status_code\": (\"resp\", \"code\"),\n55\t    \"response_time\": (\"resp\", \"roundtrip\"),\n56\t    \"response_size\": (\"resp\", \"length\"),\n57\t}\n58\t\n59\t\n60\tdef caido_url() -> str:\n61\t    return os.environ.get(\"STRIX_CAIDO_URL\", _DEFAULT_CAIDO_URL).rstrip(\"/\")\n62\t\n63\t\n64\tdef _graphql_url() -> str:\n65\t    base_url = caido_url()\n66\t    parsed = urlparse(base_url)\n67\t    if parsed.scheme not in {\"http\", \"https\"} or not parsed.netloc:\n68\t        raise ValueError(f\"Invalid Caido URL: {base_url}\")\n69\t    return f\"{base_url}/graphql\"\n70\t\n71\t\n72\tdef _login_as_guest() -> str:\n73\t    body = json.dumps({\"query\": \"mutation { loginAsGuest { token { accessToken } } }\"}).encode(\n74\t        \"utf-8\"\n75\t    )\n76\t    req = urllib.request.Request(  # noqa: S310\n77\t        _graphql_url(),\n78\t        data=body,\n79\t        headers={\"Content-Type\": \"application/json\"},\n80\t        method=\"POST\",\n81\t    )\n82\t    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=10) as resp:  # noqa: S310  # nosec B310\n83\t        payload = json.loads(resp.read())\n84\t    return str(payload[\"data\"][\"loginAsGuest\"][\"token\"][\"accessToken\"])\n85\t\n86\t\n87\tasync def _new_client() -> Client:\n88\t    token = await asyncio.to_thread(_login_as_guest)\n89\t    client = Client(caido_url(), auth=TokenAuthOptions(token=token))\n90\t    await client.connect()\n91\t    return client\n92\t\n93\t\n94\tasync def get_client() -> Client:\n95\t    \"\"\"Return the shared Caido client, creating it under a lock if needed.\n96\t\n97\t    The lock prevents two concurrent callers from each building a client and\n98\t    racing ``connect()`` on the same transport (\"Transport is already\n99\t    connected\").\n100\t    \"\"\"\n101\t    async with _CLIENT_LOCK:\n102\t        client = _CLIENT_CACHE.get(\"default\")\n103\t        if client is None:\n104\t            client = await _new_client()\n105\t            _CLIENT_CACHE[\"default\"] = client\n106\t        return client\n107\t\n108\t\n109\tasync def call_with_client[T](fn: Callable[[Client], Awaitable[T]]) -> T:\n110\t    \"\"\"Run ``fn`` against the shared client, serialized through ``_CLIENT_LOCK``.\n111\t\n112\t    The Caido GraphQL transport is not safe for concurrent use: two in-flight\n113\t    requests race and raise \"Transport is already connected\". Serializing every\n114\t    proxy call through the lock prevents that.\n115\t    \"\"\"\n116\t    async with _CLIENT_LOCK:\n117\t        client = _CLIENT_CACHE.get(\"default\")\n118\t        if client is None:\n119\t            client = await _new_client()\n120\t            _CLIENT_CACHE[\"default\"] = client\n121\t        return await fn(client)\n122\t\n123\t\n124\tasync def close_client() -> None:\n125\t    async with _CLIENT_LOCK:\n126\t        client = _CLIENT_CACHE.pop(\"default\", None)\n127\t    if client is None:\n128\t        return\n129\t    await client.aclose()\n130\t\n131\t\n132\tasync def list_requests_with_client(\n133\t    client: CaidoClient,\n134\t    *,\n135\t    httpql_filter: str | None = None,\n136\t    first: int = 50,\n137\t    after: str | None = None,\n138\t    sort_by: SortBy = \"timestamp\",\n139\t    sort_order: SortOrder = \"desc\",\n140\t    scope_id: str | None = None,\n141\t) -> Any:\n142\t    builder = client.request.list().first(first)\n143\t    if httpql_filter:\n144\t        builder = builder.filter(httpql_filter)\n145\t    if after:\n146\t        builder = builder.after(after)\n147\t    if scope_id:\n148\t        builder = builder.scope(scope_id)\n149\t    target, field = _REQ_FIELD_MAP[sort_by]\n150\t    builder = (builder.descending if sort_order == \"desc\" else builder.ascending)(target, field)\n151\t    return await builder.execute()\n152\t\n153\t\n154\tasync def get_request_with_client(\n155\t    client: CaidoClient,\n156\t    request_id: str,\n157\t    *,\n158\t    part: RequestPart = \"request\",\n159\t) -> Any:\n160\t    # The Caido SDK's generated pydantic model marks Request.raw and\n161\t    # Response.raw as required strings even though the GraphQL fragment\n162\t    # makes them conditional via `@include(if: $includeRequestRaw)`.\n163\t    # Passing False for either causes pydantic validation to fail with\n164\t    # \"Field required\" on the missing raw field. Always request both —\n165\t    # the caller picks which one to surface via ``part``.\n166\t    opts = RequestGetOptions(request_raw=True, response_raw=True)\n167\t    return await client.request.get(request_id, opts)\n168\t\n169\t\n170\tdef build_raw_request(\n171\t    *,\n172\t    method: str,\n173\t    url: str,\n174\t    headers: dict[str, str],\n175\t    body: str,\n176\t) -> tuple[ConnectionInfoInput, bytes]:\n177\t    parsed = urlparse(url)\n178\t    if not parsed.scheme or not parsed.netloc:\n179\t        raise ValueError(f\"Invalid URL: {url}\")\n180\t    is_tls = parsed.scheme.lower() == \"https\"\n181\t    host = parsed.hostname or \"\"\n182\t    port = parsed.port or (443 if is_tls else 80)\n183\t    path = parsed.path or \"/\"\n184\t    if parsed.query:\n185\t        path = f\"{path}?{parsed.query}\"\n186\t\n187\t    final_headers = {**headers}\n188\t    final_headers.setdefault(\"Host\", parsed.netloc)\n189\t    final_headers.setdefault(\"User-Agent\", \"strix\")\n190\t    if body and \"Content-Length\" not in {k.title() for k in final_headers}:\n191\t        final_headers[\"Content-Length\"] = str(len(body.encode(\"utf-8\")))\n192\t\n193\t    lines = [f\"{method.upper()} {path} HTTP/1.1\"]\n194\t    lines.extend(f\"{k}: {v}\" for k, v in final_headers.items())\n195\t    raw = (\"\\r\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\r\\n\\r\\n\" + body).encode(\"utf-8\")\n196\t    return ConnectionInfoInput(host=host, port=port, is_tls=is_tls), raw\n197\t\n198\t\n199\t_RESPONSE_BODY_MAX_CHARS = 8192\n200\t\n201\t\n202\tdef parse_raw_response(raw_bytes: bytes | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n203\t    \"\"\"Parse a raw HTTP response into the same shape ``list_requests`` emits.\n204\t\n205\t    Returns ``None`` when ``raw_bytes`` is missing or unparseable. On\n206\t    success returns ``{status_code, length, headers, body, body_truncated}``\n207\t    where ``body`` is decoded as UTF-8 (replacement chars on invalid\n208\t    bytes) and clipped at :data:`_RESPONSE_BODY_MAX_CHARS`.\n209\t    \"\"\"\n210\t    if not raw_bytes:\n211\t        return None\n212\t    try:\n213\t        head, _, body_bytes = raw_bytes.partition(b\"\\r\\n\\r\\n\")\n214\t        lines = head.decode(\"iso-8859-1\", errors=\"replace\").split(\"\\r\\n\")\n215\t        if not lines:\n216\t            return None\n217\t        status_parts = lines[0].split(\" \", 2)\n218\t        if len(status_parts) < 2 or not status_parts[1].isdigit():\n219\t            return None\n220\t        status_code = int(status_parts[1])\n221\t        headers: dict[str, str] = {}\n222\t        for line in lines[1:]:\n223\t            if \":\" not in line:\n224\t                continue\n225\t            k, v = line.split(\":\", 1)\n226\t            headers[k.strip()] = v.strip()\n227\t        body_text = body_bytes.decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\n228\t        body_truncated = len(body_text) > _RESPONSE_BODY_MAX_CHARS\n229\t        if body_truncated:\n230\t            body_text = body_text[:_RESPONSE_BODY_MAX_CHARS]\n231\t        return {\n232\t            \"status_code\": status_code,\n233\t            \"length\": len(body_bytes),\n234\t            \"headers\": headers,\n235\t            \"body\": body_text,\n236\t            \"body_truncated\": body_truncated,\n237\t        }\n238\t    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - tolerate any malformed raw bytes; None signals \"unparseable\" to the caller.\n239\t        return None\n240\t\n241\t\n242\tdef parse_raw_request(raw_content: str) -> dict[str, Any]:\n243\t    lines = raw_content.split(\"\\n\")\n244\t    request_line = lines[0].strip().split(\" \")\n245\t    if len(request_line) < 2:\n246\t        raise ValueError(\"Invalid request line format\")\n247\t    method, url_path = request_line[0], request_line[1]\n248\t\n249\t    parsed_headers: dict[str, str] = {}\n250\t    body_start = 0\n251\t    for i, line in enumerate(lines[1:], 1):\n252\t        if line.strip() == \"\":\n253\t            body_start = i + 1\n254\t            break\n255\t        if \":\" in line:\n256\t            key, value = line.split(\":\", 1)\n257\t            parsed_headers[key.strip()] = value.strip()\n258\t\n259\t    body = \"\\n\".join(lines[body_start:]).strip() if body_start < len(lines) else \"\"\n260\t    return {\"method\": method, \"url_path\": url_path, \"headers\": parsed_headers, \"body\": body}\n261\t\n262\t\n263\tdef full_url_from_components(\n264\t    original: Any,\n265\t    components: dict[str, Any],\n266\t    modifications: dict[str, Any],\n267\t) -> str:\n268\t    if \"url\" in modifications:\n269\t        return str(modifications[\"url\"])\n270\t    headers = components[\"headers\"]\n271\t    host_header = headers.get(\"Host\") or original.host\n272\t    scheme = \"https\" if original.is_tls else \"http\"\n273\t    return f\"{scheme}://{host_header}{components['url_path']}\"\n274\t\n275\t\n276\tdef apply_modifications(\n277\t    components: dict[str, Any],\n278\t    modifications: dict[str, Any],\n279\t    full_url: str,\n280\t) -> dict[str, Any]:\n281\t    headers = modifications:\n293\t        body = modifications[\"body\"]\n294\t    if \"cookies\" in modifications:\n295\t        cookies: dict[str, str] = {}\n296\t        if headers.get(\"Cookie\"):\n297\t We grab a guest token by\n5\t``session.exec()``-ing curl from inside the container, then construct\n6\ta host-side :class:`caido_sdk_client.Client` against the runtime's\n7\texposed-port URL for all subsequent SDK calls.\n8\t\"\"\"\n9\t\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport asyncio\n13\timport contextlib\n14\timport json\n15\timport logging\n16\tfrom typing import TYPE_CHECKING\n17\t\n18\tfrom caido_sdk_client import Client, TokenAuthOptions\n19\tfrom caido_sdk_client.types import CreateProjectOptions\n20\t\n21\t\n22\tif TYPE_CHECKING:\n23\t    from agents.sandbox.session import BaseSandboxSession\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n27\t\n28\t\n29\t_LOGIN_AS_GUEST_BODY = (\n30\t    '{\"query\":\"mutation LoginAsGuest { loginAsGuest { token { accessToken } } }\"}'\n31\t)\n32\t\n33\t\n34\tasync def _login_as_guest(\n35\t    session: BaseSandboxSession,\n36\t    *,\n37\t    container_url: str,\n38\t    attempts: int = 10,\n39\t) -> str:\n40\t    \"\"\"``session.exec`` curl to fetch a guest token; retry until ready.\n41\t\n42\t    Caido's GraphQL listener may not be up the instant the container\n43\t    starts. The retry loop also doubles as the Caido readiness probe —\n44\t    no separate TCP healthcheck needed.\n45\t    \"\"\"\n46\t    last_err: str | None = None\n47\t    for i in range(1, attempts + 1):\n48\t        result = await session.exec(\n49\t            \"curl\",\n50\t            \"-fsS\",\n51\t            \"-X\",\n52\t            \"POST\",\n53\t            \"-H\",\n54\t            \"Content-Type: application/json\",\n55\t            \"-d\",\n56\t            _LOGIN_AS_GUEST_BODY,\n57\t            f\"{container_url}/graphql\",\n58\t            timeout=15,\n59\t        )\n60\t Failures (missing git,\n60\t    non-repo path, detached HEAD, timeout) degrade to None so the SARIF\n61\t    emit is never blocked by a provenance lookup.\n62\t    \"\"\"\n63\t    path = Path(repo_path)\n64\t    if not path.is_dir():\n65\t        return None, None\n66\t\n67\t    def _run(args: list[str]) -> str | None:\n68\t        try:\n69\t            result = subprocess.run(  # noqa: S603\n70\t                [\"git\", \"-C\", str(path), *args],  # noqa: S607\n71\t                capture_output=True,\n72\t                text=True,\n73\t                check=False,\n74\t                timeout=5,\n75\t            )\n76\t        except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):\n77\t            return None\n78\t        if result.returncode != 0:\n79\t            return None\n80\t        return result.stdout.strip() or None\n81\t\n82\t    commit = _run([\"rev-parse\", \"HEAD\"])\n83\t    branch = _run([\"rev-parse\", \"--abbrev-ref\", \"HEAD\"])\n84\t    if branch == \"HEAD\":  # detached HEAD carries no branch name\n85\t        branch = None\n86\t    return commit, branch\n87\t\n88\t\n89\tdef get_global_report_state() -> Optional[\"ReportState\"]:\n90\t    return _global_report_state\n91\t\n92\t\n93\tdef set_global_report_state(report_state: \"ReportState\") -> None:\n94\t    global _global_report_state  # noqa: PLW0603\n95\t    _global_report_state = report_state\n96\t\n97\t\n98\tclass ReportState:\n99\t    \"\"\"Per-scan product artifact state plus artifact writer.\n100\t\n101\t    The Agents SDK owns model/tool execution, tracing, and conversation\n102\t    persistence. BYOK responses\n557\t    charge only the OpenRouter fee to ``usage.cost`` (often 0) and report the\n558\t    provider charge in ``usage.cost_details.upstream_inference_cost``, so the\n559\t    true BYOK total is the sum of the two.\n560\t    \"\"\"\n561\t    usage: Any = getattr(completion_response, \"usage\", None)\n562\t    if usage is None and isinstance(completion_response, dict):\n563\t        usage = cast(\"dict[str, Any]\", completion_response).get(\"usage\")\n564\t    if usage is None:\n565\t        return None\n566\t\n567\t    def _field(container: Any, name: str) -> Any:\n568\t usage_cost = _field(usage, \"cost\")\n574\t    if isinstance(usage_cost, int | float) and usage_cost > 0:\n575\t        total += float(usage_cost)\n576\t\n577\t    if bool(_field(usage, total += float(upstream)\n581\t\n582\t    return total if total > 0 else None\n583\t\n584\t\n585\tdef _estimate_response_cost(kwargs: Any, completion_response: Any) -> float | None:\n586\t \"model\", None)\n600\t    if not isinstance(model, str) or not model:\n601\t        return None\n602\t\n603\t    provider = None\n604\t    litellm_params = kwargs.get(\"litellm_params\") if SAME VULNERABILITY means:\n37\t   - Same root cause (e.g., \"missing input validation\" not just \"SQL injection\")\n38\t   - Same affected component/endpoint/file (exact match or clear overlap)\n39\t   - Same exploitation method or attack vector\n40\t   - Would be fixed by the same code change/patch\n41\t\n42\t2. NOT DUPLICATES if:\n43\t   - Different endpoints even with same vulnerability type (e.g., SQLi in /login vs /search)\n44\t   - Different parameters in same endpoint (e.g., XSS in 'name' vs 'comment' field)\n45\t   - Different root causes (e.g., stored XSS vs reflected XSS in same field)\n46\t   - Different severity levels due to different impact\n47\t   - One is authenticated, other is unauthenticated\n48\t\n49\t3. ARE DUPLICATES even if:\n50\t   - Titles are worded differently\n51\t   - Descriptions have different level of detail\n52\t   - PoC uses different payloads but exploits same issue\n53\t   - One report is more thorough than another\n54\t   - Minor variations in technical analysis\n55\t\n56\t4. DEPENDENCY-CVE reports use package identity:\n57\t   - Same CVE and same package/ecosystem is a duplicate\n58\t   - Same CVE but different package/ecosystem is NOT a duplicate\n59\t   - Same package/ecosystem but different CVE is NOT a duplicate\n60\t\n61\tCOMPARISON GUIDELINES:\n62\t- Focus on the technical root cause, not surface-level similarities\n63\t- Same vulnerability type (SQLi, XSS) doesn't mean duplicate - location matters\n64\t- Consider the fix: would fixing one also fix the other?\n65\t- When uncertain, lean towards NOT duplicate\n66\t\n67\tFIELDS TO ANALYZE:\n68\t- title, description: General vulnerability info\n69\t- target, endpoint, method: Exact location of vulnerability\n70\t- technical_analysis: Root cause details\n71\t- poc_description: How it's exploited\n72\t- impact: What damage it can cause\n73\t\n74\tRespond with a single JSON object and nothing else:\n75\t\n76\t{\n77\t  \"is_duplicate\": true,\n78\t  \"duplicate_id\": \"vuln-0001\",\n79\t  \"confidence\": 0.95,\n80\t  \"reason\": \"Both reports describe SQL injection in /api/login via the username parameter\"\n81\t}\n82\t\n83\tOr, if not a between 0 and 1.\n96\t- ``reason`` is a specific explanation mentioning endpoint/parameter/root cause.\n97\t- Output ONLY the JSON object — no surrounding prose, no code fences.\"\"\"\n98\t\n99\t\n100\tdef _prepare_report_for_comparison(report: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:\n101\t    relevant_fields = [\n102\t        \"id\",\n103\t        \"title\",\n104\t report_ecosystem == ecosystem:\n190\t                return {\n191\t                    \"is_duplicate\": True,\n192\t                    \"duplicate_id\": str(report.get(\"id\") or \"\")[:64],\n193\t ecosystem:\n197\t                return {\n198\t                    \"is_duplicate\": True,\n199\t                    \"duplicate_id\": str(report.get(\"id\") or \"\")[:64],\n200\t                    \"confidence\": 1.0,\n201\t                    \"reason\": \"Same dependency CVE/package identity with missing ecosystem\",\n202\t                }\n203\t            continue\n204\t\n205\t        if _report_cve(report) != cve:\n206\t            continue\n207\t        found_legacy_same_cve = True\n208\t        if _legacy_report_mentions_package(\n209\t            report,\n210\t            ecosystem=ecosystem,\n211\t            package_name=package_name,\n212\t        ):\n213\t            return {\n214\t                \"is_duplicate\": True,\n215\t                \"duplicate_id\": str(report.get(\"id\") or \"\")[:64],\n216\t                \"confidence\": 1.0,\n217\t                \"reason\": \"Same dependency CVE/package identity in legacy report\",\n218\t            }\n219\t\n220\t    if found_legacy_same_cve:\n221\t        return None\n222\t\n223\t    package_label = f\"{ecosystem}/{package_name}\" if ecosystem else package_name\n224\t    return {\n225\t        \"is_duplicate\": False,\n226\t        \"duplicate_id\": \"\",\n227\t        \"confidence\": 1.0,\n228\t        \"reason\": f\"No existing dependency report for {cve} in {package_label}\",\n229\t    }\n230\t\n231\t\n232\tdef _parse_dedupe_response(content: str) -> dict[str, Any]:\n233\t    text = content.strip()\n234\t    if text.startswith(\"```\"):\n235\t        text = text.strip(\"`\")\n236\t        if text.lower().startswith(\"json\"):\n237\t            text = text[4:]\n238\t        text = text.strip()\n239\t    start = text.find(\"{\")\n240\t    end = text.rfind(\"}\")\n241\t    if start == -1 or end == -1 or end <= start:\n242\t        raise ValueError(f\"No JSON object found in dedupe response: {content[:500]}\")\n243\t    parsed = json.loads(text[start : end + 1])\n244\t\n245\t    duplicate_id = str(parsed.get(\"duplicate_id\") or \"\")[:64]\n246\t    reason = str(parsed.get(\"reason\") or \"\")[:500]\n247\t    try:\n248\t        confidence = float(parsed.get(\"confidence\", 0.0))\n249\t    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n250\t        confidence = 0.0\n251\t\n252\t    return {\n253\t        \"is_duplicate\": bool(parsed.get(\"is_duplicate\", False)),\n254\t        \"duplicate_id\": duplicate_id,\n255\t        \"confidence\": confidence,\n256\t        \"reason\": reason,\n257\t    }\n258\t\n259\t\n260\tdef _extract_text(response: ModelResponse) -> str:\n261\t    parts: list[str] = []\n262\t    for item in response.output:\n263\t        if not isinstance(item, ResponseOutputMessage):\n264\t            continue\n265\t        for chunk in item.content:\n266\t            text = getattr(chunk, \"text\", None)\n267\t            if text:\n268\t                parts.append(text)\n269\t    return \"\".join(parts)\n270\t\n271\t\n272\tasync def check_duplicate(\n273\t    candidate: dict[str, Any], existing_reports: list[dict[str, Any]]\n274\t) -> dict[str, Any]:\n275\t    if not existing_reports:\n276\t        return {\n277\t            \"is_duplicate\": False,\n278\t            \"duplicate_id\": \"\",\n279\t            \"confidence\": 1.0,\n280\t            \"reason\": \"No existing reports to compare against\",\n281\t        }\n282\t\n283\t    dependency_duplicate = _check_dependency_duplicate(candidate, existing_reports)\n284\t    if dependency_duplicate is not None:\n285\t        return dependency_duplicate\n286\t\n287\t    try:\n288\t        settings = load_settings()\n289\t        model_name = settings.llm.model\n290\t        if not model_name:\n291\t            return {\n292\t dedupe check\",\n296\t            }\n297\t\n298\t        candidate_cleaned = _prepare_report_for_comparison(candidate)\n299\t        existing_cleaned = [_prepare_report_for_comparison(r) for r in candidate vulnerability against existing reports:\\n\\n\"\n304\t            f\"{json.dumps(comparison_data, indent=2)}\\n\\n\"\n305\t            f\"Respond with ONLY the JSON object described in the system prompt.\"\n306\t        )\n307\t\n308\t        configure_sdk_model_defaults(settings)\n309\t        resolved_model = model_name.strip()\n310\t        model = StrixProvider().get_model(resolved_model)\n311\t        response = await model.get_response(\n312\t            system_instructions=DEDUPE_SYSTEM_PROMPT,\n313\t            input=user_msg,\n314\t            model_settings=ModelSettings(\n315\t                retry=DEFAULT_MODEL_RETRY,\n316\t                include_usage=True,\n317\t
